prabhuṃ bhūtabhaviṣyasya sāmpratasya ca satpatim ॥ 1.1.2 ॥
jñānamapratimaṃ yasya vairāgyaṃ ca jagatpateḥ।

nadījalaiḥ praticchannaḥ parvataiścābhrasannibhaiḥ ॥ 1.49.95 ॥
sarvadhātuvicitraiśca maṇi vidrumabhūṣitaiḥ।

śuklapakṣe candravṛddhau samudraḥ pūryate tadā।

plakṣadvīpe tu gomedaḥ parvatastena cocyate ।। 49.132 ।।
kuśadvīpe kuśastambastasya nāmnā sa ucyate ।। 49.133 ।।
krauñcadvīpe giriḥ krauñco madhye janapadasya ha।
nyagrodhaḥ puṣkaradvīpe tatra taiḥ sa namaskṛtaḥ।
upāsate tatra devāstrayastriṃśanmaharṣibhiḥ।
sa tatra pūjyate caiva devairdevottamottamaḥ ।। 49.136 ।।
jambūdvīpātpravarttante ratnāni vividhāni ca।

lokavistāramātrantu ālokaḥ sarvato bahiḥ।
bhūrloko'tha bhuvarlokaḥ svarloko'tha mahastathā ।। 49.148 ।।
janastapastathā satya etāvān lokasaṅ‌grahaḥ।

bhūmau rasātale caiva ākāśe pavane'nale।

vistārānmaṇjalāccaiva prasaṃkhyātena caiva hi ।

sapta prakṛtayastvetā dhārayanti parasparam।

rathanīḍaḥ smṛto hyabdastvayane kūbarāvubhau ।

akṣe cakraṃ nibaddhantu dhruve tvakṣaḥ samarpitaḥ ॥ 1.51.65 ॥
sahacakro bhramatyakṣaḥ sahākṣo bhramati dhruvaḥ।
akṣaḥ sahaiva cakreṇa bhramate'sau dhruveritaḥ ॥ 1.51.66 ॥
evamartha vaśāttasya sanniveśo rathasya tu।
tathā saṃyogabhāgena saṃsiddho bāsvaro rathaḥ ॥ 1.51.67 ॥
tenā'sau taraṇirdevastarasā sarpate divi।
yugākṣakoṭisambaddhau raśmī dvau syandanasya hi ॥ 1.51.68 ॥

bhramantamanugacchetāṃ dhruvaṃ raśmī tu tāvubhau ।
kīlāsakto yathā rajjurbhramate sarvato diśam।

udveṣṭayan sa vegena maṇḍalāni tu gacchati ॥ 1.51.76 ॥

pramloceti ca vikhyātā nimloceti ca te ubhe।

viśvāvasuśca gandharvāstathaiva surabhiśca yaḥ।

bhadraistairakṣatairaśvaiḥ sarpate'sau divi kṣaye ॥ 1.52.43 ॥

yaścāsau tapate sūrye pibannambho gabhastibhiḥ।

gatistāstveva sūryasya nīcoccatve'yanakramāt।

sūryāyutānāmayutaprabhā ca namo'stu te candrasahasragocara।
namo'stu te deva hiraṇyavāsase namo'stu te deva hiraṇyabāhave ॥ 1.55.46 ॥
namo'stu te deva hiraṇyarūpa namo'stu te deva hiraṇyanābha।
namo'stu te netrasahasracitra namo'stu te deva hiraṇyaretaḥ ॥ 1.55.47 ॥
namo'stu te deva hiraṇyavarṇa namo'stu te deva hiraṇyagarbha।
namo'stu te deva hiraṇyacīra namo'stu te deva hiraṇyadāyine ॥ 1.55.48 ॥
namo'stu te deva hiraṇyamāline namo'stu te deva hiraṇyavāhine।
namo'stu te deva hiraṇyacīra namo'stu te bhairavanādanādine ॥ 1.55.49 ॥
vakrakoṭisahasreṇa grasamāna ivāparam ॥ 1.55.52 ॥
dundubhisvananirghoṣaparjanyaninadopamaḥ।
mukto hāsastadā tena nabhaḥ sarvamapūrayat ॥ 1.55.55 ॥
tadovāca mahāyogī prīto'haṃ surasattamau ॥ 1.55.56 ॥
prīto'haṃ yuvayoḥ samyagvaraṃ dajhi yathepsitam ॥ 1.55.58 ॥
yadi prītiḥ samutpannā yadi deyo varaśca nau।
bhaktirbhavatu nau nityaṃ tvayi deva sureśvara ॥ 1.55.60 ॥
evamuktvā sa bhagavāṃstatraivāntaradhīyata ॥ 1.55.61 ॥
evameṣa mayokto vaḥ prabhāvastasya yoginaḥ।
tasmai devādhipatyāya namaskāraṃ prayuṅkta ha।
mahādeva namaste'stu maheśvara namo'stu te।
kāmāṃśca labhate sarvān pāpebhyastu vimucyate ॥ 1.55.67 ॥
mahādevaprasādena uktaṃ brahma sanātanam।
ailasyādityasaṃyogaṃ somasya ca mahātmanaḥ ॥ 1.56.2 ॥
etatsarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi parvāṇi ca yathākramam ॥ 1.56.5 ॥
yadā tu candrasūryau tau nakṣatreṇa samāgatau।
dvilavaṃ kuhumātraṃ tu te ubhe tu vicārya saḥ।
sadyaḥ pakṣaratā tena saumyena madhunā ca saḥ ॥ 1.56.12 ॥
ṛturagnistu yaḥ proktaḥ sa tu saṃvatsaro mataḥ।
yasmāt prasravate somānmāsi māsi nibodhata।
evaṃ tadamṛtaṃ saumyaṃ sudhā ca madhu caiva ha ।
pibantyambumayīrde vāstrayastriṃśattu chandajāḥ।
pibanti dvikalaṃ kālaṃ pitaraste sudhāmṛtam ॥ 1.56.25 ॥
granthimanti yathā parvāṇīkṣuveṇvorbhavantyuta ॥ 1.56.30 ॥
agnayādhānakriyā yasmāt kriyate parvasandhiṣu।
vyatīpāte sthite sūrye lesorddhantu yugāntare।
yugāntarodite caiva lekhorddhaṃ śaśinaḥ kramāt ॥ 1.56.33 ॥
yasminkāle sa sīmānte sa vyatīpāta eva tu ॥ 1.56.34 ॥
yasmāttāmanupaśyanti pitaro daivataiḥ saha।
rañjanāccaiva candrasya rāketi kavayo viduḥ ॥ 1.56.37 ॥
anyonyaṃ candrasūryau tau yadā taddarśa ucyate ॥ 1.56.40 ॥
dvau kālau saṅgamaścaiva madhyāhne niṣpatedraviḥ ॥ 1.56.42 ॥
nirmucyamānayormadhye tayormaṇḍalayostu vai ॥ 1.56.43 ॥
pratipatpañcadaśyośca parvakālo dvimātrakaḥ।
daśabhiḥ pañcabhiścaiva kalābhirdivasakramāt।
devāste pitaraḥ sarve devāstān bhāvayantyuta।
pitā pitāmahaścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ।
yajvāno ye tu some na somavantastu te smṛtāḥ ॥ 1.56.61 ॥
brahmacaryeṇa tapasā yajñena prajayā ca vai ॥ 1.56.64 ॥
śraddhayā vidyayā caiva pradānena ca saptadhā।
karmasveteṣu ye yuktā bhavantyā devapātanāt ॥ 1.56.65 ॥
svargatā divi modante pitṛmantamupāsate ॥ 1.56.66 ॥
parānnāni ca lipsante kampamānāstatastataḥ ॥ 1.56.72 ॥
karambhavālukāyāñca asipatravane tathā।
bhūmyāpasavyadarbheṣu dattvā piṇḍatrayantu vai।
itye te pitaro devā devāśca pitaraśca vai।
ityarkapitṛ somānāmailasya ca samāgamaḥ।
vaiśvarūpyantu sarvasya kathitañcaikadeśikam।
svāyambhuvasya hītyeṣa sargaḥ krānto mayātra vai।
caturyugāni yānyāsan pūrvaṃ svāyambhuve'ntare ।
teṣāñcaturyugaṃ hyetat pravakṣyāmi nibodhata ॥ 1.57.2 ॥
yugañca yugabhedañca yugadharmantathaiva ca ॥ 1.57.3 ॥
yugasandhyaṃśakañcaiva yugasandhānameva ca।
laukikena pramāṇena vibuddho'bdastu mānuṣaḥ।
tenābdena prasaṅkhyāya vakṣyāmīha caturyugam ।। 57.5 .
nimeṣakālatulyaṃ hi vidyāllaghvakṣarañcayat ॥ 1.57.5 ॥
ahorātre vibhajate sūryo mānuṣadaivike।
etaddivyamahorātraṃ śāstre'smin niśvayo mataḥ ॥ 1.57.11 ॥
ye te rātryahanī divye prasaṅkhayāte tayoḥ punaḥ।

catvāri bhārate varṣe yugāni kavayo viduḥ।
dvāparaśca kali ścaiva yugānyetāni kalpayet ॥ 1.57.21 ॥
kṛtatretādiyuktā sā manorantaramucyate ॥ 1.57.32 ॥
manvantarasya saṅkhyā tu varṣāgreṇa nibodhata।
catuḥ sahasrayuktaṃ vai prathamantat kṛtaṃ yugam।
yugapatsamavetārtho dvidhā vaktuṃ na śakyate।
kramāgataṃ mayā hyetattubhyaṃ proktaṃ yugadvayam।
tatra tretāyugasyādau manuḥ saptarṣayaśca te।
satyena brahmacaryeṇa śrutena tapasā ca vai।
abhivyaktāstu te mantrāstārakādyairnidarśanaiḥ।
saptarṣibhistu te proktāḥ smārtaṃ dharmaṃ manurjagau ॥ 1.57.45 ॥
saṅkalpitena manasā vācoktena svasarmaṇā।
tretāyuge tvavikalaḥ karmmārambhaḥ prasiddhyati ॥ 1.57.52 ॥
parasparavirodhena manuntāḥ punaranvayuḥ।
priyavratottānapādau pradhamantau mahīpatī ॥ 1.57.56 ॥
yajñaḥ pravartitaścaiva tadā hyevantu daivataiḥ।
sārddhaṃ viśvabhujā caiva devendreṇa mahaujasā।
svāyambhuve'ntare devairyajñāste prāk pravartitāḥ ॥ 1.57.61 ॥
nyagrodhau tau smṛtau bāhū vyāmo nyagrodha ucyate।
vāmenaivocchrayādyasya sama ūrddhvantu dehinaḥ।
ādye kṛte ca dharmo'sti tretāyāṃ samprapadyate।
aniścayādigamanāddharmatattvaṃ nigadyate।
dharmatattve tu bhinnānāṃ matibhedo bhavennṛṇām ॥ 1.58.7 ॥
ayaṃ dharmo hyayaṃ neti niścayo nābhigamyate ॥ 1.58.8 ॥

mantrabrāhmaṇavinyāsaiḥ svaravarṇaviparyayaiḥ ॥ 1.58.12 ॥
anye tu prahitāstīrthaiḥ kecittān pratyavasthitāḥ ॥ 1.58.14 ॥
ādhvaryavasya prastāvairbahudhā vyākulaṃ kṛtam ॥ 1.58.16 ॥
vyākulaṃ dvāpare bhinne kriyate bhinnadarśanaiḥ ॥ 1.58.17 ॥
dvāpare sampravartante vinaśyanti punaḥ kalau ॥ 1.58.18 ॥
vāṅmanaḥkarmajairduḥkhairnirvedo jāyate punaḥ ।
arthaśāstravikalpaśca hetuśāstravikalpanam ॥ 1.58.23 ॥
dvāpareṣu pravarttante rogo lobho vadhastathā।
niḥśepe dvāpare tasmin tasya sandhyā tu pādataḥ ॥ 1.58.28 ॥
pratiṣṭhate guṇairhīno dharmo'sau dvāparasya tu ।
dvāparasya ca varṣe yā tiṣyasya tu nibodhata।
ete svabhāvāstiṣyasya sādhayanti ca vai prajāḥ ॥ 1.58.31 ॥
kalau pramārako rogaḥ satataṃ kṣudbhayāni vai।
na pramāṇaṃ smṛterasti tiṣye loke yuge yuge।
garbhastho mriyate kaścidyauvanasthastathāparaḥ ।
sthāvire madhyakaumāre mriyante vai kalau prajāḥ ॥ 1.58.34 ॥
saṃkṣobho jāyate'tyarthaṃ kalimāsādya vai yugam।
bhavantīha kalau tasmin śayanāsanabhojanaiḥ ॥ 1.58.39 ॥
caturāśramaśaithilyāddharmaḥ pravicaliṣyati ॥ 1.58.45 ॥
patayaśca bhaviṣyanti bahavo'smin kalau yuge।
puruṣālpaṃ bahustrīkaṃ yugānte paryupasthite ॥ 1.58.52 ॥
bahuyācanako loko bhaviṣyati parasparam।
śūdrā dharmañcariṣyanti yugānte paryupasthite ॥ 1.58.59 ॥
vedavikrayiṇaścānye tīrthavikrayiṇo'pare ।
utpadyante tathā te vai saṃprāpte tu kalau yuge।
strīvadhaṃ govadhaṃ kṛtvā hatvā caiva parasparam।
upahanyustadānyonyaṃ sādhayanti tathā prajāḥ ॥ 1.58.67 ॥
prajā tu bhrūṇahatyāyāmatha vai sampravarttate।
dhanyā dharmañcariṣyanti yugānte dvijasattamāḥ ॥ 1.58.71 ॥
gotreṇa vai candramaso nāmnā pramitirucyate।
mādhavasya tu soṃśena pūrvaṃ svāyambhuve'ntare ॥ 1.58.76 ॥
sa tadā taiḥ parivṛto mlecchān hanti sahasraśaḥ ॥ 1.58.78 ॥
pravṛttacakro balavān mlecchānāmantakṛdvibhuḥ।
mādhavasya tu soṃśena devasya hi vijajñivān ।
pūrvajanmavidhijñaiśca pramitirnāma vīryavān ॥ 1.58.85 ॥
gotreṇa vai candramasaḥ pūrve kaliyuge prabhuḥ।

upahiṃsanti cānyonyaṃ prapadyante parasparam ॥ 1.58.91 ॥

cittasammohanaṃ kṛtvā tāsāntaiḥ saptamantu tat।
bhāvino'rthasya ca balāttataḥ kṛtamavartata ॥ 1.58.102 ॥
sadā saptarṣayaścaiva tatra te ca vyavasthitāḥ ॥ 1.58.104 ॥
kalijaiḥ saha te sarve nirviśeṣāstadābhavan ॥ 1.58.105 ॥
tatasteṣu kriyāvatsu varttante vai prajāḥ kṛte ।

āpādato mastakantu navatālo bhavettu yaḥ।

taiḥ śiṣṭaiścarito dharmaḥ samyageva yuge yuge ॥ 1.59.35 ॥

dānaṃ trividhamityetat kaniṣṭhajyeṣṭhamadhayamam ।
ṛṣibhirdharmatattvajñaiḥ pūrve svāyambhuve'ntare ॥ 1.59.55 ॥
atra vo varttayiṣyāmi vidhirmanvantarasya yaḥ ।
itaretaravarṇasya cāturvarṇasya caiva hi।

guṇasāmye varttamāne sarvasampralaye tadā।

bhūtabhedāstu bhedebhyo jajñire te parasparam।

prayujyate hyaśvamedhastena vā yujyate tu saḥ ॥ 1.60.23 ॥

iti niścitya manasā buddhiṃ cakre janādhipaḥ ।। 60.36 ।।
tasmai tadupanītaṃ hi vidyāvittaṃ dvijottamāḥ ।। 60.38 ।।
manasā gatavittāste mamedaṃ dhanamityuta।
tathānyastatra vai vidvān brahmavāhasutaḥ kaviḥ।
yājñavalkyo mahātejāstapasvī brahmavittamaḥ ।। 60.41 ।।
brahmaṇo'ṅgāt samutpanno vākyaṃ provāca susvaram।

tejastvabhyadhikaṃ tasya nityameva vivasvataḥ।
dvau sutau tu mahāvīryau kanyāṃ kālindimeva ca ॥ 2.22.37 ॥
tato yamo yamī caiva yamajau saṃbabhūvatuḥ ॥ 2.22.38 ॥
ahaṃ yāsyāmi bhadrante svameva bhavanaṃ pituḥ ॥ 2.22.41 ॥
tvayeha bhavane mahyaṃ vastavyaṃ nirviśaṅkayā।
imau ca bālakau mahyaṃ kanyā ca varavarṇinī ॥ 2.22.42 ॥
bhartre vai naivamākhyeyamidaṃ bhagavate tvayā।
ādityo janayāmāsa putrāvādityavarcasau ॥ 2.22.49 ॥
pūrvajasya manostulyau sādṛśyena tu tau prabhū ।
manustaccākṣamatsarvaṃ yamastadvai na cākṣamat ॥ 2.22.53 ॥
manunā saha dharmmātmā pituḥ sarvvaṃ nyavedayat ॥ 2.22.57 ॥
śapto'hamasmiँllokeśa jananyā tapatāṃ vara।
tava prasādo nastrātuṃ hyetasmānmahato bhayāt ॥ 2.22.59 ॥
vivasvānevamuktastu yamaṃ provāca vai prabhuḥ।
yena tvāmāviśatkrodho dharmmajñaṃ satyavādinam।
na śakyametanmithyā tu karttuṃ māturvacastava ॥ 2.22.61 ॥
tulyeṣvapyadhikaḥ sneha ekasmin kriyate tvayā ॥ 2.22.64 ॥
sā tatpariharantī vai nācacakṣe vivasvataḥ।
ātmanā sa samādhāya yogantathyamapaśyata ॥ 2.22.65 ॥
sā tatsarvaṃ yathātattvamācacakṣe vivasvataḥ ॥ 2.22.66 ॥
vivasvānatha tacchrutvā kruddhastvaṣṭāramabhyayāt।
anukūlaṃ bhavedevaṃ yadi syāt samayo mataḥ ॥ 2.22.70 ॥
rūpaṃ vivasvatastvāsīttiryagūrdhvamadhastathā ॥ 2.22.71 ॥
tattu nirbhāsitantejastejasāpahṛtena tu ॥ 2.22.74 ॥
dharmmeṇa rañjayāmāsa dharmmarājastatastu saḥ ।
so'labhat karmaṇā tena śubhena paramadyutiḥ ॥ 2.22.81 ॥
bhāvyasau nāgate tasmin manuḥ sāvarṇike'ntare।
merupṛṣṭe suramye vai adyāpi carate prabhuḥ ॥ 2.22.83 ॥
bhrātā śanaiścarastatra grahatvaṃ sa tu labdhavān।
yastu jyeṣṭo mahātejāḥ sargo yasya tu sāmpratam।
vistaraṃ tasya vakṣyāmi manorvaivasvatasya ha ॥ 2.22.86 ॥
tato manvantare'tīte cākṣuṣe daivataiḥ saha।
tasya vaivasvato vakṣye sāmpratasya mahātmanaḥ।
manoḥ prathamajasyāsan navaputrāstu tatsamāḥ ॥ 2.23.3 ॥
brahmaṇā tu manuḥ pūrvaṃ coditastu nibodhata।
anugacchāmi bhadrante tamilā pratyuvāca ha ॥ 2.23.8 ॥
dharmmayuktamidaṃ vācyaṃ putrakāmaṃ prajāpatim।
saivamuktvā punarddevī tayorantikamāgamat ॥ 2.23.10 ॥
manunaivāhamuktāsmi anugacchasva māmiti।
anena tava dharmajñe praśrayeṇa damena ca ॥ 2.23.13 ॥
satyena caiva suśroṇi prītau svo varavarṇini।
sudyumna iti vikhyātastriṣu lokeṣu pūjitaḥ।
jagatpriyo dharmmaśīlo manorvaṃśa vivarddhanaḥ ॥ 2.23.15 ॥
mānavaḥ sa tu sudyumnaḥ strībhāvamagamat prabhuḥ।
budhenāntaramāsādya maithunāyopamantritā।
utkalaśca gayaścaiva vinatāśvastathaiva ca।
daśadhā taddadhat kṣetramakarot pṛthivīmimām ॥ 2.23.20 ॥
pratiṣṭā dharmarājasya sudyumnasya mahātmanaḥ ॥ 2.23.22 ॥
mānavaḥ sa tu sudyumnaḥ strībhāvamagamat punaḥ ॥ 2.23.23 ॥
etacchrutvā tu ṛṣayaḥ papracchustadanantaram ।
mānavaḥ sa tu sudyumnaḥ strībhāvamagamat katham ॥ 2.23.24 ॥
pṛṣadhro hisayitvā tu gurorgāvamabhakṣayat ॥ 2.24.1 ॥
maruttaścakravarttī sa nariṣyantamavāptavān।
kevalastasya putrastu bandhumān kevalātmajaḥ।
atha bandhumataḥ putro dharmātmā vegavān nṛpaḥ ॥ 2.24.14 ॥
budho vegavataḥ putra stṛṇabindurbudhātmajaḥ।
sucandra iti vikhyāto hemacandrādanantaram।
somadattasya rājarṣeḥ sutobhūjjanamejayaḥ।
janamejayātmajaścaiva pramatirnāma viśrutaḥ ॥ 2.24.21 ॥
revasya raivataḥ putraḥ kakujhī nāma dhārmikaḥ।
kanyayā saha śrutvā ca gandharvaṃ brahmaṇo'ntike।
muhūrttaṃ devadevasya mārtyaṃ bahuyugaṃ vibhoḥ ॥ 2.24.26 ॥
bhojavṛṣṭyandhakairguptāṃ vasudevapurogamaiḥ।
dattvā jagāma śikharaṃ merostapasi saṃsthitaḥ ॥ 2.24.29 ॥
kathaṃ bahuyuge kāle vyatīte sūtanandana।
na ca rogaḥ prabhavati brahmalokagatasya hi ॥ 2.24.34 ॥
brahmadānañca navamaṃ prājāpatyamanantaram ॥ 2.24.43 ॥
sāvitramarddhasāvitraṃ sarvvato madrameva ca ॥ 2.24.45 ॥

manoharamadhātryañca gandharvānugataśca yaḥ ॥ 2.24.47 ॥
uttarādisvarasyaiva brahmā vai devatā'tra ca।
manudeśasamutpannā mūrcchanā śuddhamadhyam।
tatraikaḥ saṃcarasthāyī sacarāstacarībhavan।
kumāramaparaṃ vidyādvistaraṃ vamanaṃ gatam।
śyenastu aparohastu uttaraḥ parikīrttitaḥ।
viparyayasvaro'pi syādyasya durghaṭito'pi na ॥ 2.25.14 ॥

yathātmano hyalaṅkāro viparyasto'tigarhitaḥ।

evameva hyalaṅkāro viparyasto vigarhitaḥ ॥ 2.25.25 ॥
yathoddiṣṭasya mārgasya karttavyasya vidhīyate ॥ 2.25.26 ॥

pañcamo madhyamaścaiva dhaivate tu niṣādajaiḥ।

aṅgadarśanamityāhurmāne dve samake tathā।
caturthamuttare caiva madravatyāṃ ca madrake।
madrake dakṣiṇasyāpi yathoktā varttate kalā ॥ 2.25.42 ॥
pūrvvamevānuyogantu dvitīyā buddhiriṣyate।
ekatvamupayogasya dvayoryaddhi dvijottama।
kakujhinastu taṃ lokaṃ raivatasya gatasya ha।
āgate sa vikukṣau tu samāṃse sahasainike।
tatheti codito rājñā vidhivatsamupasthitaḥ।
ikṣvākustu tataḥ kruddho vikukṣimidamabravīt।
prāptaḥ paramadharmmātmā sa cāyodhyādhipo'bhavat ॥ 2.26.20 ॥
indrasya vṛṣabhūtasya kakutstho jāyate purā ॥ 2.26.24 ॥
pūrvvamāḍībake yuddhe kakutsthastena sa smṛtaḥ ।
anenāstu kakutsthasya pṛthuromā ca sa smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.26.25 ॥
dhundhuvadhaṃ mahāprājña śrotumicchami vistarāt।
prayātaṃ tamuttaṅkastu brahmarṣiḥ pratyavārayat ॥ 2.26.33 ॥
bhavato rakṣaṇaṃ kāryyaṃ tattāvat kartumarhati।
nirudvignastapaḥ kartuṃ na hi śaknomi pārthiva ॥ 2.26.34 ॥
antarbhūmigatastatra vālukāntarhito mahān ॥ 2.26.36 ॥
saṃvatsarasya paryyante sa niḥśvāsaṃ pramuñcati।
tasya niḥśvāsavātena raja uddhūyate mahat।
lokāḥ svasthā bhavantvadya tasmin vinihate'sure ।
tvaṃ hi tasya vadhāyādya samarthaḥ pṛthivīpate ॥ 2.26.42 ॥
viṣṇunā ca varo datto mama pūrvvaṃ tato'nagha।
na hi dhundhurmahāvīryyastejasālpena śakyate ॥ 2.26.43 ॥
vīryyaṃ hi sumahattasya devairapi durāsaham ॥ 2.26.44 ॥
evamuktastu rājarṣiruttaṅkena mahātmanā।
rājā saṃnyastaśastro'hamayantu tanayo mama।
sa taṃ vyādiśya tanayaṃ dhundhumāraṇamudyatam।
jagāma parvvatāyaiva tapase śaṃsitavrataḥ ॥ 2.26.47 ॥
kubalāśvastu dharmmātmā piturvacanamāsthitaḥ।
prāyāduttaṅka sahito dhundhostasya nivāraṇe ॥ 2.26.48 ॥
tasmin prayāte durddharṣe divi śabdo mahānabhūt।
adyaprubhṛtyeṣa nṛpo dhundhumāro bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.26.50 ॥
sagatvā puruṣa vyāghrastanayaiḥ saha vīryyavān ॥ 2.26.51 ॥
tasya putraiḥ khanadbhiśca vālukāntarhitastadā ॥ 2.26.53 ॥
mukhajenāgninā kruddho lokānudvarttayanniva ॥ 2.26.54 ॥
vāri śuśrāva yogena mahodadhirivodaye।
somasya somapaśreṣṭha dhārormikalilo mahān ॥ 2.26.55 ॥
tasya vārimayaṃ vegamapibat sa narādhipaḥ।
dharmme ratiñca satataṃ svarge vāsaṃ tathākṣayam।
haryaśvasya nikumbho'bhūt kṣatradharmarataḥ sadā ॥ 2.26.62 ॥
vikhyātā triṣu lokeṣu putrastasyāḥ prasenajit ॥ 2.26.64 ॥
atyantadhārmiko gaurī tasya patnī pativratā ॥ 2.26.65 ॥
tasyāstu gaurikaḥ putra ścakravarttī babhūva ha ॥ 2.26.66 ॥
yāvatsūrya udayati yāvacca pratitiṣṭhati।
purukutsamambarīṣaṃ mucukundañca viśrutam।
rāvaṇena hato yena trilokīvijaye purā ॥ 2.26.75 ॥
trasadaśvo'naraṇyasya haryyaśvastasya cātmajaḥ ।
tasya putro'bhavadrājā tridhanvā nāma dhārmmikaḥ।
tasya satyavrato nāma kumāro'bhūnmahābalaḥ।
tena bhāryyā vidarbhasya hṛtā hatvā divaukasaḥ ॥ 2.26.78 ॥
tamadharmmeṇa saṃyuktaṃ pitā trayīguṇo'tyajat।
apadhvaṃseti bahuśo'vadat krodhasamanvitaḥ ॥ 2.26.81 ॥
pitā cainamathovāca śvapākaiḥ saha varttaya ॥ 2.26.82 ॥
ityuktaḥ sa nirākrāmannagarādvacanādvibho ॥ 2.26.83 ॥

so'bhavadghālavo nāma gale baddho mahātapāḥ।
tasya vratena bhaktyā ca kṛpayā ca pratijñayā।
viśvāmitrakalatrañca babhāra vinaye sthitaḥ ॥ 2.26.91 ॥
piturniyogātabhajannṛpe tu vanamāsthite ॥ 2.26.93 ॥
satyavratastu bālyāttu bhāvino'rthasya vai balāt।
evaṃ satyavratastān vai kṛtavān saptame pade ॥ 2.26.97 ॥
jānan dharmmān vasiṣṭhastu na ca mantrānihecchati।

kulasya niṣkṛtiḥ svasya kṛteyañca bhavediti ।। 26.101 ।।

sagarastu suto bāhorjajñe saha gareṇa vai।

vanaṃ praviśya dharmmātmā saha patnyā tapo'carat ।। 26.128 ।।
kasyacittvatha kālasya toyārthaṃ prasthito nṛpaḥ।
vṛddhatvāddurbbalatvācca antarā sa mamāra ca ।। 26.129 ।।
sapatnyā tu garastasyai datto garbhajighāṃsayā ।। 26.130 ।।
āseduśca tatastasmiṃstadantaste mahārṇave ।। 26.145 ।।
tasya cakṣuḥ samāsādya tejastat pratipadyate।

dve patnyau sagarasyāstāṃ tapasā dagdhakilbiṣe।

yo'sau niveśayāmāsa purandevapuropamam ॥ 2.27.1 ॥

garbhamutsṛjase na tvaṃ vipraḥ prāha bṛhaspatiḥ ।। 28.36 ।।
satyaṃ brūhi sutaḥ kasya somasyātha bṛhaspateḥ ।। 28.39 ।।

somasyeti mahātmānaṃ kumārandasyuhantamam ।। 28.42 ।।
budha ityakaronnāma tasya putrasya dhīmataḥ ।। 28.43 ।।
pratipūrvvañca gamane samabhyuttiṣṭhate budhaiḥ।
etatsomasya vai janma kīrttitaṃ dvijasattamāḥ।
somasya janma śrutvaiva sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ।। 28.49 ।।
somasya tu budhaḥ putro budhasya tu purūravāḥ।
atīva putro lokeṣu rūpeṇāpratimo'bhavat ।। 29.3 ।।
vane caitrarathe ramye tathā mandākinītaṭe।
eteṣu vanasukhyeṣu surairācariteṣu ca।
anagnadarśanañcaiva akāmāta saha maithunam ।। 29.11 ।।
deśe puṇyatame caiva maharṣibhiralaṃkṛte ।। 29.46 ।।
tasya putrā babhūvurhi ṣaḍindropamatejasaḥ।
suhotrasyābhajjahnuḥ keśikāgarbhasambhavaḥ।
pratigatya tato gaṅgā vitato yajñakarmmaṇi ।। 29.51 ।।
jahnuśca dayitaṃ putraṃ suhotraṃ nāma dhārmikam।
kuśastambastapastepe putrārthī rājasattamaḥ।
pūrṇe varṣasahasre vai śatakratumapaśyata ।। 29.60 ।।
putratvaṃ kalpayāmāsa svayameva purandaraḥ।
paurukutsābhavadbhāryā gādhistasyāmajāyata।
tasyāṃ putrastu vai bharttā bhārgavo bhṛgunandanaḥ।
putrārthe sādhayāmāsa caruṃ gādhestathaiva ca ।। 29.64 ।।
ajeyaḥ kṣatriyairyuddhe kṣatriyarṣabhasūdanaḥ ।। 29.66 ।।
tapasyābhirato nityamaraṇyaṃ praviveśa ha ।। 29.68 ।।
bhartturvacanamavyagrā hṛṣṭā mātre nyavedayat ।। 29.70 ।।
mātā tu tasyai daivena duhitre svaṃ caruṃ dadau ।
atha satyavatī garbhaṃ kṣatriyāntakaraṃ śubham।
viśvaṃ hi brahma tapasā mayā tatra samarpitam ।। 29.75 ।।
brāhmaṇāpasadastvanya ityukto munirabravīt ।। 29.76 ।।
punaḥ satyavatī vākyamevamuktābravīdidam।
kāmamevaṃvidhaḥ putro mama syāttu vada prabho ।। 29.79 ।।
mayyanyathā na śakyaṃ vai kartumeva dvijottama।
tataḥ prasādamakarot sa tasyāstapaso balāt ।। 29.80 ।।
putre nāsti viśeṣo me pautre vā varavarṇini।
tvayā yathoktaṃ vacanaṃ tathā bhadre bhaviṣyati ।। 29.81 ।।
tapasyabhiratandāntaṃ jamadagniḥ śamātmakam ।। 29.82 ।।
yamanādvaiṣṇavasyāgnerjamadagnirajāyata ।। 29.83 ।।
jamadagnistato vīryyājjajñe brahmavidāṃ varaḥ।
viśvāmitrasya putrastu śunaḥśepho'bhavanmuniḥ।
hariścandrasya yajñe tu paśutve niyutaḥ savai।
devairdattaḥ sa vai yasmāddevarātastato'bhavat ।। 29.91 ।।
madhucchando nayaścaiva kṛtadevau dhruvāṣṭakau ।। 29.92 ।।
babhravaḥ pāṇinaścaiva dhyāvajapyāstathaiva ca ।। 29.95 ।।
pauroravasya puṇyasya brahmarṣeḥ kauśikasya tu ।। 29.98 ।।
aṣṭakasya suto yo hi prokto jahnugaṇomayā ।। 29.99 ।।
evamuktastato vākyamabravīdidamarthavat।
anyāyopagatairdravyairāhṛtya yajane dhiyā।
dharmābhikāṃkṣī yajate na dharmaphalamaśnute ।। 29.102 ।।
tena dattāni dānāni aphalāni bhavantyuta।
tasya dharmmapravṛttasya hiṃsakasya durātmanaḥ ।। 29.105 ।।
evaṃ labdhvā dhanaṃ mohāddadato yajataśca ha।
kāmānanabhisandhāya yajate ca dadāti ca ।। 29.107 ।।
sa dānaphalamāpnoti tacca dānaṃ sukhodayam।
dānena bhogānāpnoti svargaṃ satyena gacchati ।। 29.108 ।।
suduścaraṃ nāma tapo yena taptaṃ mahatpurā।
tasyorddhvaretasastatra sthitasyānimiṣaspṛham।
somatvantanurāpede mahābuddhiḥ sa vai dvijaḥ ॥ 2.28.4 ॥
ūrddhvamācakrame tasya somatvaṃ bhāvitātmanaḥ।
sa tābhyaḥ sahasaivātha digbhyo garbhaḥ prabhānvitaḥ।
patantaṃ somamālokya brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ।
sa hi devamayo viprā dharmmārthī satyasaṅgaraḥ।
yukto vājisahasreṇa siteneti hi naḥ śrutam ॥ 2.28.10 ॥
tasminnipatite devāḥ putre'treḥ paramātmani।
tatraivāṅgirasastasya bhṛgoścaivātmajastathā।
ṛgbhiryajurbhirbahubhiratharvāṅgirasairapi ॥ 2.28.12 ॥
samena rathamukhyena sāgarāntāṃ vasundharām।
tasya yaccāpi tattejaḥ pṛthivīmanvapadyata।
sa labdhatejāstapasā saṃstavaistaiśca karmmabhiḥ।
tatastasmai dadau rājyaṃ brahmā brahmavidāṃ varaḥ।
hiraṇyagarbhaścodgātā brahmā brahmatvameyivān।
sadasyastatra bhagavān harirnārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ ।
sanatkumārapramukhairādyairbrahmarṣibhirvṛtaḥ ॥ 2.28.23 ॥
tebhyo brahmarṣimukhyebhyaḥ sadasyebhyaśca vai dvijāḥ ॥ 2.28.24 ॥
tena snehena bhagavān rudrastasya bṛhaspateḥ।
tena brahmarṣimukhyebhyaḥ paramāstraṃ mahātmanā।
tatra tadyuddhamabhavat pratyakṣantārakāmayam।
garbhamutsṛjase na tvaṃ vipraḥ prāha bṛhaspatiḥ ॥ 2.28.36 ॥
satyaṃ brūhi sutaḥ kasya somasyātha bṛhaspateḥ ॥ 2.28.39 ॥
yadatra tathyantad‌brūhi tāre kasya sutastvayam ॥ 2.28.41 ॥
somasyeti mahātmānaṃ kumārandasyuhantamam ॥ 2.28.42 ॥
budha ityakaronnāma tasya putrasya dhīmataḥ ॥ 2.28.43 ॥

sa dānaphalamāpnoti tacca dānaṃ sukhodayam।
dānena bhogānāpnoti svargaṃ satyena gacchati ॥ 2.29.108 ॥
putro gṛtsamadasyāpi śunako yasya śaunakaḥ।
dharmmaśca dīrghatapaso vidvān dhanvantaristataḥ।
kathaṃ dhanvantarirddevo mānuṣeṣviha jajñivān।
etadveditumicchāmastato brūhi priyaṃ tathā ॥ 2.30.8 ॥
ajastvamiti hovāca tasmādajastu saḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.30.10 ॥
vidhatsva bhāgaṃ sthānañca mama loke surottama ॥ 2.30.11 ॥
kṛto yajñavibhāgastu yajñiyaurhe suraistathā ॥ 2.30.12 ॥
vedeṣu vidhiyuktañca vidhihotraṃ maharṣibhiḥ।
na śakyamiha homo vai tulyaṃ karttuṃ kadācana ॥ 2.30.13 ॥
arvāksuto'si he deva nāmamantro'si vai prabho।
tenaiva ca śarīreṇa devatvaṃ prāpsyasi prabho।
cārumantrairghṛtairgandhairyakṣyanti tvāṃ dvijātayaḥ ॥ 2.30.15 ॥
atha ca tvaṃ punaścaiva āyurvedaṃ vidhāsyasi।
tasmāt tasmai varaṃ dattvā viṣṇurantardaghe tataḥ ॥ 2.30.17 ॥
putrakāma stapastepe nṛpo dīrghatapāstathā ॥ 2.30.18 ॥
vareṇa cchandayāmāsa prīto dhanvantarirnṛpam ॥ 2.30.19 ॥
bhagavan yadi tuṣṭastvaṃ putro me dhṛtimān bhava।
tatheti samanujñāya tatraivāntaradhīyata ॥ 2.30.20 ॥
tasya gehe samutpanno devo dhanvantaristadā।
dhanvantarisutaścāpi ketumāniti viśrutaḥ।
atha ketumataḥ putro vibho bhīmaratho nṛpaḥ।
divodāsa iti khyāto vārāṇasyadhipo'bhavat ॥ 2.30.23 ॥
etasminneva kāle tu kṛtadāro maheśvaraḥ।
jugupsate sā nityañca devaṃ devīṃ tathaiva ca ॥ 2.30.31 ॥
tathoktastu mahādevaḥ sarvāṃllokānavekṣya ha।
pūjā tu mahatī caiva nityameva prayujyate।
gandhairdhūpaiśca mālyaiśca prekṣaṇīyaistathaiva ca ॥ 2.30.41 ॥
annapradānayuktaiśca atyadbhutamivābhavat।
evaṃ sampūjyate tatra nityameva gaṇeśvaraḥ ॥ 2.30.42 ॥
na prayacchati putrāṃstu nikumbhaḥ kāraṇena tu।
na dadāti ca putraṃ me kṛtaghno bahubhojanaḥ ॥ 2.30.49 ॥
prahasyaināmathovāca avimuktaṃ hi me gṛham ॥ 2.30.57 ॥
tasmāttadavimuktaṃ hi proktaṃ devena vai svayam ॥ 2.30.58 ॥
yasmin vasati vai devaḥ sarvadevanamaskṛtaḥ।
antarddhānaṃ kalau yāti tatpurantu mahātmanaḥ।
antarhite pure tasmin purī sā vasate punaḥ ॥ 2.30.60 ॥
bhadraśreṇyasya putrastu durdamo nāma nāmataḥ।
tena putreṇa bālena prahṛtaṃ tasya vai punaḥ ॥ 2.30.64 ॥
pratarddanasya putrau dvau vatso gargaśca viśrutaḥ ॥ 2.30.65 ॥
vatsaputro hyalarkastu sannatistasya cātmajaḥ ।
suketutanayaścāpi dharmaketuriti śrutiḥ।
dharmaketostu dāyādaḥ satyaketurmahārathaḥ ॥ 2.30.71 ॥
satyaketusutaścāpi vibhurnāma prajeśvaraḥ।
suvibhustu vibhoḥ putraḥ sukumārastataḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.30.72 ॥
gārgyasya gargabhūmistu vātsyo vatsasya dhīmataḥ ॥ 2.30.74 ॥
ityete kāśyapāḥ proktā rajerapi nibodhata।
rājeyamiti vikhyātaṃ kṣatramindrabhayāvaham ॥ 2.30.76 ॥
tadā daivāsure yuddhe samutpanne sudāruṇe।
āvayorbhagavān yuddhe vijetā ko bhaviṣyati।
brūhi naḥ sarvvalokeśa śrotumicchāmahe vayam ॥ 2.30.78 ॥
rajiryatastato lakṣmīryato lakṣmīstato dhṛtiḥ।
yato dhṛtistato dharmo yato dharmastato jayaḥ ॥ 2.30.80 ॥
abhyayurjayamicchantaḥ stuvanto rājasattamam ॥ 2.30.81 ॥
indro bhavāmi dharmātmā tato yotsyāmi saṃyuge ॥ 2.30.83 ॥
asmākamindraḥ prahlādastasyārthe vijayāmahe।
sa tatheti bruvanneva devairapyabhicoditaḥ।
bhaviṣyasīndro jitveti devairapi nimantritaḥ ॥ 2.30.85 ॥
taṃ tathā tu rajhiṃ tatra devaissaha śatakratuḥ ॥ 2.30.87 ॥
rajiputro'hamityuktvā punarevābravīdvacaḥ।
yasyāhamindraputraste khyātiṃ yāsyāmi śatruhan ॥ 2.30.88 ॥
samakrāmanta bahudhā svargalokaṃ triviṣṭapam ॥ 2.30.91 ॥
hataujā durbalo mūḍho rajiputraiḥ prasīda me ॥ 2.30.94 ॥
prayatiṣyāmi devendra taddhitārthaṃ mahādyute।
tataḥ karmma cakārāsya tejaḥsaṃvarddhanaṃ mahat ॥ 2.30.97 ॥
teṣāñca buddhisaṃmohamakarodbuddhisattamaḥ।
tato lebhe suraiśvaryamaindrasthānaṃ tathottamam ॥ 2.30.99 ॥
āhavan taṃ marutsomamannakāmaḥ prajeśvaram।
tena te marutastasya marutsomena toṣitāḥ।
annaṃ tasya sakṛtpakvamahorātre na kṣīyate।
yatidharmamavāpyeha brahmabhūyāya te gatāḥ ॥ 2.31.6 ॥
sañjayasya jayaḥ putro vijayastasya jagmivān ॥ 2.31.8 ॥
vijayasya jayaḥ putrastasya haryandvataḥ smṛtaḥ ।
haryandutastato rājā sahadevaḥ pratāpavān ॥ 2.31.9 ॥
sahadevasya dharmātmā adīna iti viśrutaḥ।
adīnasya jayatsenastasya putro'tha saṃkṛtiḥ ॥ 2.31.10 ॥
ityete kṣatra dharmāṇo nahuṣasya nibodhata ॥ 2.31.11 ॥
yaduṃ ca turvasuṃ caiva devayānirvyajāyata ॥ 2.31.16 ॥
rathantasmai dadau rudraḥ prītaḥ paramabhāsvaram।
yuktaṃ mano javairaśvairyena kanyāṃ samudvahat।
sa tena rathamukhyena jigāya ca tato mahīm ॥ 2.31.19 ॥
yayātiryudhi durddharṣo devadānavamānavaiḥ ।
yovatsudeśaprabhavaḥ kauravo janamejayaḥ।
durbuddhirhiṃsayāmāsa lohagandhaṃ narādhipam ॥ 2.31.22 ॥

paurajānapadaistyakto na lebhe śarma karhicit ॥ 2.31.23 ॥
tataḥ sa duḥkhasantapto nālabhatsaṃvidaṃ kvacit।
yojayāmāsa cendrotaḥ śaunako janamejayam।
sa lohagandho vyanaśattasyāvasathametya ha।
sa ca divyo rathastasmādvasoścedipatestathā ॥ 2.31.26 ॥
tato hatvā jarāsandhaṃ bhīmastaṃ rathamuttamam।
pradadau vāsudevāya prītyā kauravanandanaḥ ॥ 2.31.27 ॥
tvaṃ yado pratipadyasva pāpmānaṃ jarayā saha।
tasmājjarānna te rājan grahītumahamutsahe ॥ 2.31.32 ॥
aśaktaḥ kāryakaraṇe paribhūtastu yauvane।
santi te bahavaḥ putrā mattaḥ priyatarā nṛpa।
sa evamukto yadunā tīvrakopasamanvitaḥ।
māmanādṛtya durbuddhe yadāttha navadeśika ॥ 2.31.37 ॥
yastvaṃ me hṛdayājjāto vayaḥ svaṃ na prayacchasi ॥ 2.31.38 ॥
turvaso pratipadyasva pāpmānaṃ jarayā saha ॥ 2.31.39 ॥
tasmājjarāṃ na te rājan grahītumahamutsahe ॥ 2.31.40 ॥
yastvaṃ me hṛdayājjāto vayaḥ svanna prayacchasi।
tasmāt prajā samucchedaṃ turvaso tava yāsyati ॥ 2.31.41 ॥
guru dāraprasakteṣu tiryagyonigateṣu vā।
druhyo tvaṃ pratipadyasva varṇarūpavināśinīm।
jarāṃ varṣasahasraṃ vai yauvanaṃ svandadasva me ॥ 2.31.45 ॥
pūrṇe varṣasahasre te pratidāsyāmi yauvanam।
yastvaṃ me hṛdayājjāto vayaḥ svanna prayacchasi।
tasmād druhyo priyaḥ kāmo na te sampatsyate kvacit ॥ 2.31.48 ॥
nauplavottarasañcārastatra nityaṃ bhaviṣyati।
ano tvaṃ pratipadyasva pāpmānaṃ jarayā saha।
evaṃ varṣasahasrantu careyaṃ yauvanena te ॥ 2.31.50 ॥
yastvaṃ me hṛdayājjāto vayaḥ svanna prayacchasi।
jarādoṣastvayokto'yaṃ tasmātte pratipatsyate ॥ 2.31.52 ॥
agnipraskandanaparastvaṃ cāpyeva bhaviṣyasi ॥ 2.31.53 ॥
evamuktaḥ pratyuvāca putraḥ pitaramañjasā।
yathānumanyase tāta kariṣyāmi tathaiva ca ॥ 2.31.57 ॥
jarayāhaṃ praticchanno vayorūpadharastava।
yauvanaṃ bhavate dattvā cariṣyāmi yathārtavat ॥ 2.31.59 ॥
dharmmāvirodhādrājendro yathārhati sa eva hi ॥ 2.31.63 ॥
devānatarpayadyajñaiḥ pitṝñchrāddhaistathaiva ca।
dharmmeṇa ca prajāḥ sarvvā yathāvadanurañjayan।
avirodhena dharmasya cacāra sukhamuttamam ॥ 2.31.67 ॥
viśvāsaheto reme vai vaibrāje nandane vane ॥ 2.31.68 ॥
sevitā viṣayāḥ putra yauvanena mayā tava ॥ 2.31.72 ॥
yadurjyeṣṭhastava suto jātastamanu turvasuḥ।
mātā pitrorvacanakṛtsa hi putraḥ praśasyate ।
yadunāhamavajñātastathā turvasunāpi ca।
putro yastvānuvartteta sa rājā te mahāmate ॥ 2.31.84 ॥

pūrṇe dhūmavrate cāpi ghore varṣasahasrike ।

tasmāttvaṃ tapasā buddhyā śrutena ca balena ca ॥ 2.35.156 ॥
tejasā cāpi vibudhān sarvānabhibhaviṣyasi।
yacca kiñcinmama brahman vidyate bhṛgunandana ॥ 2.35.157 ॥
pratibhāsyati te sarvaṃ taccādyantaṃ na kasyacit ॥ 2.35.158 ॥
ajeyatvaṃ dhaneśatvamavadhyatvaṃ ca vai dadau।
harṣāt prādurbabhau tasya devastotraṃ maheśvaram।
tadā tiryaksthitastvevaṃ tuṣṭuve nīlalohitam ॥ 2.35.161 ॥
ririhāṇāya lopāya vatsarāya jagatpate ॥ 2.35.162 ॥
kapardine hyūrddhvaromṇe hayāya karaṇāya ca।
vasuretāya rudrāya tapase cīravāsase ॥ 2.35.164 ॥
hrasvāya muktakeśāya senānye rohitāya ca ॥ 2.35.165 ॥
suvṛttāya suhastāya dhanvine bhārgavāya ca ॥ 2.35.166 ॥
sahasrabāhave caiva sahasrāmalacakṣuṣe।
sahasrakukṣaye caiva sahasracaraṇāya ca ॥ 2.35.167 ॥
sahasraśirase caiva bahurūpāya vedhase।
pinākine ceṣumate citrāya rohitāya ca ॥ 2.35.171 ॥
dundubhyāyaikapādāya arhāya buddhaye tathā।
kapilāyaikavīrāya mṛtyave tryambakāya ca ॥ 2.35.173 ॥
vṛtadhanvane kavicine rathine ca varūthine।
bhūtabhavyabhavāyaiva tubhyaṃ kālātmane namaḥ।
viśvāya marute caiva tubhyandevātmane namaḥ।
agnisomartvigijyāya paśumantrauṣadhāya ca ॥ 2.35.196 ॥
tapase caiva satyāya tyāgāya ca śamāya ca ॥ 2.35.197 ॥
janastapāya satyāya tubhyaṃ lokātmane namaḥ ॥ 2.35.199 ॥
avyaktāyātha mahate bhūtāyaivondriyāya ca ।
śuddhāya vibhave caiva tubhyaṃ nityātmane namaḥ ॥ 2.35.201 ॥
satyānteṣu mahānteṣu caturṣu ca namo'stu te ॥ 2.35.202 ॥
namaḥstotre mayā hyasmin sadasadvyāhṛtaṃ vibho।
madbhakta iti brahmaṇya sarvantat kṣantumarhasi ॥ 2.35.203 ॥
brahmeti praṇatastasmai prāñjalirvākyamabravīt ॥ 2.36.1 ॥
tataḥ so'ntarhite tasmin deveśānucare tadā।
anayā satataṃ bhaktyā praśrayeṇa damena ca।
snehena caiva suśroṇi prīto'smi varavarṇini ॥ 2.36.5 ॥

taṃ te saṃpūrayāmyadya yadyapi syāt sudurlabham ॥ 2.36.6 ॥
adṛśyaṃ sarvabhūtaistu saṃprayogamihecchasi ॥ 2.36.9 ॥
devendrānalavarṇābhe varārohe sulocane।
tataḥ svagṛhamāgamya jayantyā sahitaḥ prabhuḥ ॥ 2.36.11 ॥
dākṣiṇyaṃ tasya tadbudhvā pratijagmuryathāgatam ॥ 2.36.14 ॥
buddhvā tadantaraṃ so'tha daityānāmiva coditaḥ।
tataste hṛṣṭamanaso vidyārthamupapedire ॥ 2.36.18 ॥
vibhrāntaprekṣite sādhvi trivarṇāyatalocane ॥ 2.36.21 ॥
evamuktā'bravīddevī bhaja bhaktān mahāvrata।
eṣa brahman satāṃ dharmo na dharmaṃ lopayāmi te ॥ 2.36.22 ॥
anugacchata māṃ sarve tyajatainaṃ bṛhaspatim ॥ 2.36.27 ॥
śrutvā tasya tataste vai saṃmantryārthavaco'bruvan ॥ 2.36.30 ॥
eṣa vai gururasmākamantarepsurayaṃ dvijaḥ ॥ 2.36.31 ॥
sthitā vayaṃ nideśe'sya gacchatvaṃ sādhu mā ciram ॥ 2.36.34 ॥
evamuktvāsurāḥ sarve prāpadyanta bṛhaspatim।
yadā na pratipadyante tenoktaṃ tanmahaddhitam ॥ 2.36.35 ॥
cukopa bhārgavasteṣāmavalepena vai tadā।
aho dhig vañcitāḥ smeha parasparamathābruvan ॥ 2.36.39 ॥
tato'surāḥ paritrastā devebhyastvaritā yayuḥ।
tatastenāvalepena gatā yūthaṃ parābhavam ॥ 2.36.43 ॥
prahlādastamathovāca mānaṃ tvaṃ tyaja bhārgava।
yadi nastvaṃ na kuruṣe prasādaṃ bhṛgunandana।
apadhyātā stvayā hyadya pravekṣyāmo rasātalam ॥ 2.36.46 ॥
evamukto'nunītaḥ sa stutaḥ kopaṃ nyayacchata ॥ 2.36.47 ॥
uvācedanna bhetavyaṃ na gantanyaṃ rasātalam।
avaśyambhāvī hyartho'yaṃ prāpto vo mayi jāgrati ॥ 2.36.48 ॥
matprasādācca yuṣmābhirbhuktaṃ trailokyamūrjjitam ॥ 2.36.50 ॥
sāvarṇike punastubhyaṃ rājyaṃ kila bhaviṣyati।
evaṃ kilamahaṃ proktaḥ pautraste brahmaṇā svayam।
tathāhṛteṣu lokeṣu tapo'sya na kilābhavat ॥ 2.36.53 ॥
tasmādajena prītena dattaṃ sāvarṇike'ntare ॥ 2.36.54 ॥
prītena cāmaratvaṃ vai dattaṃ tubyaṃ svayambhuvā।
tasmānnirutsu kastvaṃ vai paryyāyaṃ saha mākulaḥ ॥ 2.36.56 ॥
na ca śakyaṃ mayā tubhyaṃ purastādvai visarpitum ।
imau ca śiṣyau dvau mahyaṃ tulyāvetau bṛhaspateḥ ।
ajayannasurā devān bhagnā devā amantrayan ॥ 2.36.62 ॥
vadhyamānāstadā devairviviśuste rasātalam।
tataḥ prabhṛti śāpena bhṛgunaimittikena ca ॥ 2.36.68 ॥
karttuṃ dharmmavyavasthānamadharmmasya ca nāśanam ॥ 2.36.69 ॥
prahlādasya nideśe tu ye'surā na vyavasthitāḥ।
yajñaṃ pravartayāmāsa caitye vaivasvate'ntare ॥ 2.36.71 ॥
prādurbhāve tadānyasya brahmaivāsīt purohitaḥ।
sambhūtaḥ sa samudrāntarhiraṇyakaśiporvadhe।
yajamānantu daityendramadityāḥ kulanandanaḥ।
trailokyasya bhavān rājā tvayi sarvvaṃ pratiṣṭitam।
dātumarhasi me rājan vikramāṃstrīniti prabhuḥ ॥ 2.36.76 ॥
vāmanantaṃ ca vijñāya tato'numuditaḥ svayam ॥ 2.36.77 ॥
tribhiḥ kramairviśvamidaṃ jagadākrāmata prabhuḥ ॥ 2.36.78 ॥
saputrapautrānasurān pātālatalamānayat ॥ 2.36.80 ॥
tasya gātre jagatsarvamātmānamanupaśyati ।
tadvai rūpamupendrasya devadānavamānavāḥ।
virocana kulaṃ sarvaṃ pātāle sanniveśitam ॥ 2.36.85 ॥
tataḥ sarvāmaraiśvaryaṃ dattvendrāya mahātmane।
tretāyuge tu daśame dattātreyo babhūva ha।
māndhātuścakravarttitve tasthau tathyapuraḥsaraḥ ॥ 2.36.89 ॥
devakyāṃ vasudevāttu brahnagārgyapuraḥsaraḥ ॥ 2.36.93 ॥
aprameyo niyojyaśca yatra kāmacaro vaśī।

paraṃ paramametasmādviśvarūpānna vidyate ॥ 2.36.95 ॥
anukarṣan sarvasenāṃ hastyaśvarathasaṅkulām।
nātyarthaṃ dhārmikā ye ca ye ca dharmadviṣaḥ vkacit।
pravṛttacakrobalavān mlecchānāmantakṛdbalī।
gātreṇa vai candrasamaḥ pūrṇe kaliyuge'bhavat।
tataḥ sa vai tadā kalkiścaritārthaḥ sasainikaḥ।
karmaṇā nihatā ye tu siddhāste tu punaḥ svayam ॥ 2.36.115 ॥
tato vyatīte kalkau tu sāmānyaiḥ saha sainikaiḥ ॥ 2.36.117 ॥
turvasostu pravakṣyāmi pūrordruhyoranostathā ॥ 2.36.125 ॥
turvasostu suto vahnirvahnergobhānurātmajaḥ।
gobhānostu suto vīrastrisānuraparājitaḥ ॥ 2.37.1 ॥
karandhamastrisānostu maruttasya na cātmajaḥ।
anavatyo maruttastu sa rājāsīditi śrutam।
duṣkṛtaṃ pauravaṃ cāpi sarve putramakalpayan ॥ 2.37.3 ॥
pāṇḍyaśca keralaścaiva colaḥ kulyastathaiva ca।
druhyostu tanayau vīrau babhruḥ setuśca viśrutau।
aruddhaḥ setuputrastu bābhravo ripurucyate ॥ 2.37.7 ॥

ghṛtasya durdamo jajñe pracetāstasya cātmajaḥ ।
mahāmanāstu putrau dvau janayāmāsa viśrutau ॥ 2.37.17 ॥

uśadratho mahābāhustasya hemaḥ suto'bhavat ॥ 2.37.24 ॥
saṃgrāme cāpyajeyatvaṃ dharme caiva prabhāvanā ॥ 2.37.29 ॥
trailokyadarśanañcaiva prādhānyaṃ prasave tathā।

caturo niyatān varṇān tvaṃ vai sthāpayiteti ca।

ayaṃ hi me mahāgarbho rodate'ti bṛhaspate।
amogharetāstvañcāpi na māṃ bhajitamarhasi।

evamuktastayā samyag bṛhattejā bṛhaspatiḥ।
utsṛjantaṃ tadā reto garbhasthaḥ so'bhyabhāṣata ॥ 2.37.41 ॥
māmevamuktavān mohāttamo dīrghaṃ pravekṣyasi ॥ 2.37.44 ॥

sa tena nigṛhītastu na cacāla padātpadam।
na mayāsāditastāta balavaṃstvadvidhaḥ kvacit।
tryambakaṃ vahatā devaṃ yato jāto'smi bhūtale ॥ 2.37.50 ॥
evamukto'bravīdenaṃ jīvastvaṃ me kva yāsyasi ॥ 2.37.51 ॥
kāryākāryaṃ na vai vijho gamyāgamyaṃ tathaiva ca।
tato yavīyasaḥ patnīmautathyasyābhyamanyata।
provāca dīrghatamasaṃ krodhāt saṃrakta locanaḥ।
durvṛttastvaṃ tyajāmyeva gaccha tvaṃ svena karmaṇā।

tenāsi tvaṃ parityakto durācāro'si me matiḥ ॥ 2.37.61 ॥
karmaṇyasmistataḥ krūre tasya buddhirajāyata।
nirbhatsya caiva bahuśo bāhubhyāṃ parigṛhya ca।
koṣṭe samudre prakṣipya gaṅgāmbhasi samutsṛjat ॥ 2.37.62 ॥
taṃ sastrīko balirnāma rājā dharmārthatattvavit।
antaḥpure jugopainaṃ bhakṣyairbhojyaiśca tarpayan ॥ 2.37.64 ॥

apārthivasudevantu bālyādvyasaninaṃ nṛpam ॥ 2.37.335 ॥

etānyuktāni vai samyak saptasaptasu vai mayā।
sāvarṇasya pravakṣyāmi manorvaivasvatasya ha।
bhaviṣyasya bhaviṣyanti samāsāttu nibodhata ॥ 2.38.9 ॥
ritastapaśca śukraśca dyutirjyotiḥ prabhākarau ॥ 2.38.14 ॥
prabhurvibhurvibhāsaśca jetā hantā'rihā rituḥ।
damo dātā vidaḥ somo vittavaidyau yamo nidhiḥ।

jajñire manavaste hi bhaviṣyānāgatāntare ॥ 2.38.29 ॥
prācetasasya dakṣasya dauhitrā manavastu ye।

prajābhistapasā caiva vistaraṃ teṣu vakṣyate।
manvantarādhikāreṣu vartante ca sakṛt sakṛt।
punaruktā bahutvāttu vakṣye na teṣu vistaram।
manvantareṣu bhāvyeṣu bhūteṣvapi tathaiva ca ॥ 2.38.40 ॥
vairājastamupāsīnaṃ dharmeṇa ca bhavena ca।
dakṣa kanyā taveyaṃ vai janayiṣyati suvratā।
brahmaṇo vacanaṃ śrutvā dakṣo dharmo bhavastadā।
upabhogasamarthaiśca sadyojātaiḥ śarīrakaiḥ ॥ 2.38.47 ॥
saṃrabdhā vai vyakarṣanta mama putro mametyuta ॥ 2.38.48 ॥
abhidhyānānmanotpannānūcurvai te parasparam।
yo yasya vapuṣā tulyo bhajatāṃ sa tu taṃ sutam ॥ 2.38.49 ॥
dhruvaṃ rūpaṃ pituḥ putraḥ so'nurudhyati sarvadā।
ruceḥ prajāpateḥ putro raucyo nāmābhavatsutaḥ ॥ 2.38.54 ॥
bhūtyāmutpādito yastu bhautyo nāmābhavatsutaḥ।
vaivasvate'ntare rājā dvau manū tu vivasvatoḥ ॥ 2.38.55 ॥
prathamaṃ merusāvarṇerdakṣaputrasya vai manoḥ।
sambhūtāśca mahātmānaḥ sarve vaivasvate'ntare ॥ 2.38.59 ॥
dakṣaputrasya putrāste rohitasya prajāpateḥ।
vājiyo vājijiccaiva prabhṛtiśca kakudyatha।
tejasmānnathavo dvau tu dvādaśaite marīcayaḥ।
varṇastathāpyaṅgaviśvau muraṇyo vrajano mataḥ।
amito dravaketuśca jambhosthājasraśakrakāḥ ॥ 2.38.64 ॥
sutapāḥ paulavaścaiva saptaite rohitāntare।
daśame tvatha paryāye dharmaputrasya vai manoḥ।
dvitīyasya tu sāvarṇerbhāvyasyaivāntare manoḥ ॥ 2.38.70 ॥
devāste vai bhaviṣyanti dharmaputrasya vai manoḥ ॥ 2.38.72 ॥
paulaho hyagnitejāśca devā hyekādaśe'ntare ॥ 2.38.83 ॥
dvādaśe tvatha paryāye rudraputrasya vai manoḥ ॥ 2.38.85 ॥
rajaścaiva tu rājaśca svarṇapādastathaiva ca ॥ 2.38.90 ॥
vikramaśca kramaścaiva nibhṛtaḥ kānta eva ca।।
varyyoditastathā jiṣṭo varcasvī dyutimān haviḥ।
ete saptarṣayaḥ siddhā anye sāvarṇike'ntare।
mitravān mitrabinduśca mitraseno hyamitrahā।
devairdevāstrayastriṃśatpṛthaktvena nibodhata ॥ 2.38.103 ॥
divaspatirmahāsattvasteṣāmindro bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.38.105 ॥
paulahastattva darśī ca bhārgavaśca nirutsakaḥ।
niṣprakampastathātreyo nirmohaḥ kaśyapastathā ॥ 2.38.107 ॥
citraseno vicitraśca tapo dharmadhṛto bhavaḥ ॥ 2.38.108 ॥
raucyasyaite manoḥ putrā hyantare tu trayodaśe ॥ 2.38.109 ॥
caturddaśe tu paryāye bhautasyāpyantare manoḥ ।
trailokye yāni sattvāni gatimanti dhruvāṇi ca।
sarvaśaḥ svairguṇaistāni indrāste'bhibhavanti vai ॥ 2.38.114 ॥
ojasvī subalaścaiva bhautyasyaite manoḥ sutāḥ ॥ 2.38.116 ॥
eko vaivasvataścaiva sāvarṇo manurucyate ॥ 2.38.117 ॥
raucyo bhautyaśca yau tau tu manoḥ paulahabhārgavau।
bhautyasyaivādhipatye tu pūrṇaḥ kalpastu pūryyate ॥ 2.38.118 ॥
ante'nekayuge tasmin kṣīṇe saṃhāra ucyate ॥ 2.38.119 ॥
saptaite bhārgavā devā ante manvantare tadā।
bhuktvā trailokyamadhyasthā yugākhyāṃ hyekasaptatim ॥ 2.38.120 ॥
anādhāramidaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ vai bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.38.122 ॥
tatasteṣu vyatīteṣu trailokyasyeśvareṣviha।
manvantareṣu sarvveṣu devāste vai mahaujasaḥ ॥ 2.38.125 ॥
tatra yugasahasrāntamaharyardbrahmaṇo viduḥ।
naimittikaḥ prākṛtiko yaścaivātyantiko'rthataḥ।
brāhmo naimittikastasya kalpadāhaḥ prasaṃyamaḥ।
aharante prakurute sargasya pralayaṃ punaḥ।
tato yugasahasrānte saṃprāpte ca yugakṣaye।
tānyevātra pralīyante bhūmitvamupayānti ca।
asahyaraśmirbhagavān pibannambho gabhastibhiḥ।
bhūya eva vivartante vyāpnuvanto vanaṃ śanaiḥ।
tasmādudakaṃ sūryyasya tapato'ti hi kathyate।
tasmādapaḥ piban yā vai dīpyate ravirambare ॥ 2.38.142 ॥
tasya te raśmayaḥ sapta pibantyambho mahārṇavāt।
caturlokamimaṃ sarvaṃ dahanti śikhinastadā ॥ 2.38.144 ॥
khaṃ samāvṛtya tiṣṭhanti nirdahanto vasundharām ॥ 2.38.146 ॥
adhaścordhvaśca tiryak ca saṃruddhaṃ sūryaraśmibhiḥ ॥ 2.38.148 ॥
ekatvamupayātānāmekajvālaṃ bhavatyuta ॥ 2.38.149 ॥
caturlokamidaṃ sarvaṃ nirdahatyāśu tejasā ॥ 2.38.150 ॥
tatastāni pralīyante bhūmitvamupayānti ca ॥ 2.38.153 ॥
sarvvaṃ tadbhasmasāccakre sarvvātmā pāvakastu saḥ ॥ 2.38.154 ॥
samudrebhyo nadībhyaśca pātālebhyaśca sarvataḥ।
pibannapaḥ samiddho'gniḥ pṛthivīmāśrito jvalan ॥ 2.38.155 ॥
lokān saṃharate dīpto ghoraḥ saṃvarttako'nalaḥ ॥ 2.38.156 ॥
udatiṣṭhañchikhāstasya bahvayaḥ saṃvarttakasya tu।
tadā dahati sandīpto golakaṃ caiva sarvvaśaḥ ॥ 2.38.159 ॥
bhūrlokantu bhuvarlokaṃ svarlokañca mahastathā।
indragopanibhāḥ keciduttiṣṭhanti ghanā divi।
tadā jaladharāḥ sarve pūrayanti nabhaḥsthalam ॥ 2.38.168 ॥
tataste jaladā varṣaṃ muñcanti ca mahodyamam।
plāvayanti jagatsarvaṃ bṛhajjālaparisravaiḥ ॥ 2.38.172 ॥
anye tu salilaughaistu velāmabhibhavantyapi।
prasarantyo vrajantyekaṃ salilākhyāṃ bhajantyuta ॥ 2.38.180 ॥
bhasma sarvvamanuprāpya tasmādambho nirucyate ॥ 2.38.182 ॥
nānātve caiva śīghre ca dhāturvai ara ucyate।
tasmin yugasahasrānte divase brahmaṇo gate।
sahasrabāhuḥ prathamaḥ prajāpatistrīyapathe yaḥ puruṣo nirucyate ॥ 2.38.186 ॥
hiraṇyagarbhaḥ puruṣo mahān vai saṃpadyate vai tamasaḥ parastāt ॥ 2.38.187 ॥
caturyugasahasrānte sarvataḥ salilaplute।
janalokavivarttantastapasā labdhacakṣuṣaḥ।
tataste vasate rātriṃ tamasyekārṇave jale ॥ 2.38.194 ॥
tato rātrikṣaye prāpte pratibuddhaḥ prajāpatiḥ।
evaṃ saloke nirvṛtte upaśānte prajāpatau।
brahmanaimittike tasmin kalpite vai prasaṃyame ॥ 2.38.196 ॥
devarṣimanuvaryyeṣu tasmin saṅkalane tadā ॥ 2.38.197 ॥
kalpādāvaprataptāni janamevāśrayanti vai ॥ 2.38.198 ॥
jane tānyupapadyante yāvatsaṃplavate jagat ॥ 2.38.199 ॥
yathā sūryasya loke'sminnudayāstamanaṃ smṛtam।
pratyāhāre ca sarge ca gatimanti dhruvāṇi ca ॥ 2.38.205 ॥
vyaktā vyakto mahādevastasya sarvamidaṃ jagat ॥ 2.38.207 ॥
pūrvaprayātena tathā hyapo'nyāstenaiva tenaiva tu saṃvrajanti ॥ 2.38.208 ॥
yathā śūbhena tvaśubhena caiva tatraiva tatraiva vivarttamānāḥ।
martyāstu dehāntarabhāvitattvādravervaśādūrddhvamadhaścaranti ॥ 2.38.209 ॥
saha prajñānisargeṇa saha devaiścaturddaśa ॥ 2.38.211 ॥
etadbrāhmamaho jñeyaṃ tasya saṃkhyāṃ nibodhata।
muhūrttāstu punastriṃśadahorātramiti sthitiḥ।
māgadhenaiva mānena jalaprastho vidhīyate।
samāhani ca rātrau ca muhūrtto vai dvinālikau ॥ 2.38.221 ॥
etaddivyamahorātramiti śāstraviniścayaḥ।
etacchrutvā tu ṛṣayo vismayaṃ paramādbhutam।
ete rātryahanī pūrvaṃ kīrttite tviha taukike।
salilenāplute loke naṣṭe sthāvarajaṅgame ॥ 2.38.234 ॥
nirāloke pradagdhe tu naiśena tu samāvṛte ।
ahorātrastathaivāsya krameṇa parivarttate।
trailokye yāni satvāni gatimanti dhruvāṇi ca।
etāvān sthitikālastu hyajasyeha prajāmateḥ।
sthityante pratisargasya brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭinaḥ ॥ 2.38.240 ॥
tathaivaṃ pratisargeṇa brahmā samupaśāmyati ॥ 2.38.241 ॥
mahatpralīyate'vyakte guṇasāmyaṃ tato bhavet ॥ 2.38.242 ॥
brahmanaimittiko hyeṣa saṃprakṣālanasaṃyamaḥ ॥ 2.38.243 ॥
te devaiḥ saha tiṣṭhanti maharlokanivāsinaḥ|
ṛṣibhi rdaivataiścaiva saha gandharvarākṣasaiḥ || 39.3 ||
taistathyakāribhiryuktaiḥ śraddhāvadbhiradarpitaiḥ|
maharloketi yatproktaṃ mātariśvaṃstvayā vibho|
pratiloke ca karttavyamanekaiḥ samadhiṣṭitāḥ || 39.7 ||
provāca madhuraṃ vākyaṃ yathātattvena tattvavit || 39.9 ||
vyaktāni tu pravakṣyāmi sthānānyetāni sapta vai|
satyantu saptamo loko nirālokastataḥ param|
dvitīyaṃ bhuva ityukta antarikṣaṃ tato'bhavat|
vyāhāraistribhiretaistu brahmalokamakalpayat|
tato bhūḥ pārthivo loka antarikṣaṃ tato'bhavat|
svarloko vai divaṃ hyetatpurāṇe niścayaṃ gatam|
vāyurbhuvasyādhipatistena vāyurbhuvapatiḥ|
bhavyasya sūryo'dhipatistena sūryo divaspatiḥ || 39.22 ||
maheti vyāhṛtenaivaṃ maharlokastato'bhavat|
janastu pañcamo lokastasmājjāyanti vai janāḥ |
kalpadagdhe tadā loke pratiṣṭhanti tadā tapaḥ || 39.25 ||
brahmalokastataḥ satyaṃ saptamaḥ sa tu bhāskaraḥ || 39.27 ||
svarlokavāsinaḥ sarve devā bhuvinivāsinaḥ || 39.28 ||

tān sarvān sapta sūryāste arccirbhirnidahanti vai || 39.34 ||
bhṛguḥ pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kraturityevamādayaḥ || 39.35 ||
prajānāṃ patayaḥ sarve varttante tatra taiḥ saha|

ṣaṣṭe kāle nivarttante tattadāharviparyaye || 39.38 ||
satyantu saptamo loko hyapunarmārgagāminām|
sūryadhruvāntaraṃ yacca svargaloko divaḥ smṛtaḥ|

yojayitvā tadā sarvve varttante yogadharmmiṇaḥ || 39.78 ||
brahmakāyamavarttanta punarāvṛttidurllabham ||. 39.79 ||

svacchandagatayaḥ siddhāḥ siddhaiścānyaurvibodhitāḥ|
ebhiḥ saha mahātmā hi devadevo maheśvaraḥ|
īśvarasyottamaṃ puṇyamaṣṭamantvaupasargikam || 39.325 ||
yo gandhena samṛddhaṃ vai paramaṃ paramātmanaḥ || 39.326 ||
svena māhātmyayogena sahasrasyāmitaujasaḥ || 39.327 ||
yasya bhakteṣvasaṃmoho hyanukampārthameva ca|
vibhūtirbhrājate'tyarthaṃ devadevasya veśmani || 39.329 ||
tatsarvaṃ nikhilenedaṃ vaktrādamṛtanisravam || 39.330 ||
apītvā khalu sarvasya bhaktyāsmābhistu suvratāḥ|
praśnaṃ devavara prāṇa yathāvadvaktumarhasi || 39.331 ||
sa khalūvāca bhagavānkiṃ bhūyo varttayāmyaham|
kimavasthā bhavantyete tanno brūhi yathārthavat || 39.334 ||
mahābhūtavināśānte pralaye pratyupasthite || 39.339 ||
viṣṇunā saha saṃyuktaṃ karoti vikaroti ca|
sa rudro yaḥ sāmamayastathaiva ca yajurmayaḥ || 39.346 ||
ananyamanaso bhūtvā prapadyante maheśvaram || 39.349 ||
prapadya parayā bhaktyā jñānayuktena cetasā|
amadyapaśca yaḥ śūdro bhavabhakto jitendriyaḥ || 39.353 ||
madyapo madyapaiḥ sārddhaṃ bhūtasaṅghaiśca modate|
upasthite mahāghore hyapratyakṣe tu kasyacit ॥ 2.40.3 ॥
ante drumasya yamprāpte paścimasya manostadā ।
ante kaliyuge tasminkṣīṇe saṃhāra ucyate ॥ 2.40.4 ॥
samprakṣāle tadā vṛtte pratyāhāre hyupasthite।
svabhāvakārite tasminpravṛtte pratisañcare ॥ 2.40.6 ॥
āttagandhā tato bhūmiḥ pralayatvāya kalpate ।
praviṣṭe gandhatanmātre toyāvasthā dharā bhavet ॥ 2.40.7 ॥
sarvamāpūrayitvedaṃ tiṣṭhanti vicaranti ca ॥ 2.40.8 ॥
apāmasti guṇo yastu jyotiṣe līyate rasaḥ।
graste ca salile tejaḥ sarvvatomukhamīkṣyate ॥ 2.40.10 ॥
athāgniḥ sarvato vyāpta ādatte tajjalantadā।
sarvamāpūryyate'rcirbhistadā jagadidaṃ śanaiḥ ॥ 2.40.11 ॥
arcirbhiḥ santate tasmiṃstiryagūrddhvamadhastataḥ ।
pralīyate tadā tasmindīpārciriva mārute ॥ 2.40.12 ॥
upaśāmyati tejo hi vāyunā dhūyate mahat ॥ 2.40.13 ॥
nirāloke tadā loke vāyubhūte ca tejasi।
arūpamarasasparghamagandhaṃ na ca mūrtimat ॥ 2.40.16 ॥
bhūtendriyeṣu yughapadbhūtādau saṃsthiteṣu vai।
buddhirmanaśca liṅgaśca mahānakṣara eva ca।
vinivṛtte tadā sarge prakṛtyāvasthitena vai ।
āgatāgatikatvācca grahaṇaṃ tanna vidyate ॥ 2.40.24 ॥
bhāvagrāhyānumānācca cintayitvedamucyate।
sthite tu kāraṇe tasminnitye sadasadātmike ॥ 2.40.25 ॥
evaṃ saptādayo'bhyastātkramātprakṛtayastu vai ॥ 2.40.26 ॥
pratyāhāre tadā sarge praviśyanti parasparam।
saptadvīpasamudrāntaṃ saptalokaṃ saparvatam।
etau saṃhāravistārau brahmāvyaktau tataḥ punaḥ ॥ 2.40.31 ॥
sṛjate grasate caiva vikārānsargasaṃyame।
avyaktaṃ kṣetramityāhurbrahma kṣetrajña ucyate।
avyūhā śaṅkarāccaiva jyotirvacca vyavasthitaḥ।
yasmātpratiśarīraṃ hi sukhaduḥkhopalabdhitā।
nivarttate tadā tasya sthitirāgaḥ svayambhuvaḥ।
sahasā yojyakaiḥ sarvairbrahmaloka nivāsibhiḥ ॥ 2.40.40 ॥
tattvaprasaṃyamo hyeṣa smṛto hyāvarttako dvijāḥ ॥ 2.40.47 ॥
ūrddhva bhāvo hyadhobhāvo sukhaduḥkhe priyāpriye ॥ 2.40.48 ॥
sarvametatprayātasya guṇamātrātmakaṃ smṛtam।
ye jayanti punardehe dehānyatve tathaiva ca ॥ 2.40.51 ॥
karaṇaiḥ svaiḥ pracīyete kāyatveneha jantubhiḥ ॥ 2.40.52 ॥
sarge ca pratisarge ca saṃsāre caiva jantavaḥ।
saṃyujyante viyujyante karaṇaiḥ sañcaranti ca ॥ 2.40.53 ॥
tayoḥ pravarttakaṃ madye ihaivāvarttakaṃ rajaḥ ॥ 2.40.55 ॥
ityevaṃ parivarttante trayaḥ srotoguṇātmakāḥ।
tama sābhibhavājjanturyāthātathyaṃ na vindati।
atatddarśanātso'tha trividhaṃ badhyate tataḥ ॥ 2.40.58 ॥
prākṛtena ca bandhena tathā vaikārikena ca।
dakṣiṇābhistṛtīyena baddho'tyantaṃ vivarttate ॥ 2.40.59 ॥
anitye nityasaṃjñā ca duḥkhe ca sukhadarśanam ॥ 2.40.60 ॥
karmmajastu punardeho mahāduḥkhaṃ pravarttate ॥ 2.40.62 ॥
tailapālīkavajjīvastatraiva pari varttate ॥ 2.40.64 ॥
jñānādvijayate sarvaṃ tyāgād‌buddhirvirajyate।
vairāgyācchuddhyate cāpi śuddhaḥ sattvena mucyate ॥ 2.40.66 ॥
brahmādau sthāvarānte vai saṃsāre hyādhibhautike।
eṣa mārgo hi niradhitiryagyonau ca kāraṇam।
tiryagyonigatañcaiva kāraṇaṃ sa nirucyate ॥ 2.40.71 ॥
anaiśvaryyantu tatsarvaṃ pratighātātmakaṃ smṛtam ।
sattvasthamātrakaṃ cittaṃ yathā sattvapradarśanāt।
tena baddhasya vai bandho mokṣo muktasya tena ca ।
saṃsāre vinivṛtte tu mukto liṅgena mucyate ॥ 2.40.76 ॥
niḥsambandho hyacaitanyaḥ svātmanyevāvatiṣṭhate।
svātmavyavasthitaścāpi virūpākhyena likhyate ॥ 2.40.77 ॥
sa cāpi trividhaḥ prokto mokṣo vai tattvadarśibhiḥ ॥ 2.40.78 ॥
nirañjanatvācchuddhastu tato netā na vidyate।
anityamaśivaṃ duḥkhamiti buddhyānucintya ca।
tataḥ prayāṇakāle hi doṣairnaimittikaistathā ॥ 2.40.86 ॥
śaityātprakupito vāyurūrddhvantu kramate tataḥ ॥ 2.40.88 ॥
śarīraṃ prajahaṃso vai nirucchvāsastato bhavet।
yatheha loke khadyotaṃ nīyamānamitastataḥ ।
tiryyagūrddhvamadhastācca dhāvato'pi yathākramam।
paryyāyavācakaiḥ śabdairekārthaiḥ so'bhilikhyate।
yatheṣṭaṃ pariniryyāti bhinne dehe sunirvṛte ॥ 2.40.103 ॥
prakṛtiṃ satyamityāhu rvikāro'nṛtamucyate ॥ 2.40.106 ॥
kṣetrapratyayato yasmātkṣetrajñaḥ śubha ucyate ॥ 2.40.108 ॥
tameva ca vikārantu yasmādvai kṣarate punaḥ।
sukhaduḥkhamohabhāvā bhojyamityabhidhīyate।
acetatvāddhi viṣayastaddhi dharmmavibhuḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.40.113 ॥
na kṣīyate na kṣarati vikāraprasṛtantu tat।
yasmātpuryyanuśete ca tasmātpuruṣa ucyate।
purapratyayiko yasmātpūruṣetyabhidhīyate ॥ 2.40.115 ॥
puruṣaṃ kathayasvātha kathantajjñairvibhāṣyate।
asti nāstīti so'nyo vā baddho mukto gataḥ sthitaḥ।
nairhetikāntanirddeśyasūktastasminna vidyate ॥ 2.40.117 ॥
bhāvagrāhyamanumānyaṃ cintayanna pramuhyate ॥ 2.40.118 ॥
yajjñātvā na nivarttante kṣetrajñe tu nirañjane।
apratarkyamacintyatvādavāpyatvācca sarvaśaḥ।
vyape(ye)tasukhaduḥkhe ca niruddhe śāntimāgate ॥ 2.40.127 ॥
nirātmake punastasminvācyāvācyo na vidyate।
etau saṃhāravistārau vyaktāvyaktau tataḥ punaḥ ॥ 2.40.128 ॥
sṛjate grasate caiva grastaḥ paryavatiṣṭhate।
adhiṣṭānapravṛttena tasya te buddhipūrvakam।
sādharmyavaidharmyakṛtasaṃyogo vidhitastayoḥ।
yāvacca sargapratisargakālastāvacca tiṣṭhati susannirudhya।
pūrvaṃ hitavye tadabuddhipūrvaṃ pravarttate tatpuruṣārthameva ॥ 2.40.131 ॥
anādyanantā hyabhimānapūrvakaṃ vitrāsayantī jagadabhyupaiti ॥ 2.40.132 ॥
ukto hyasmiṃstadātyantaṃ kavibhistatpramucyate ॥ 2.40.133 ॥
ityeṣa pratisargovastrividhaḥ kīrttito mayā।।
atyadbhutāni karmmāṇi vidhimāndharmmaniścayaḥ।
vicitrāśca kathāyogā janma cāgryamanuttamam ॥ 2.41.3 ॥
papracchuḥ satriṇaḥ sarve punaḥ sargapravarttanam ॥ 2.41.5 ॥
bandheṣu sampralīneṣu guṇasāmye tamomaye ॥ 2.41.6 ॥
apravṛttau brahmaṇastu mahāsāyujyagaistadā ।
kathaṃ prapatsyate sargastannaḥ prabrūhi pṛcchatām ॥ 2.41.7 ॥
vyākhyātumupacakrāma punaḥ sargapravarttanam ॥ 2.41.8 ॥
ahaṃ vau varttayiṣyāmi yathā sargaḥ prapatsyate।
tasmādvāco nivarttante hyaprāpya manasā saha ॥ 2.41.10 ॥
avyaktavat parokṣatvādghrahaṇaṃ taddurāsadam।
sattvamātrātmako dharmmo guṇasattve pratiṣṭitaḥ।
tamomātrātmako'dharmmo guṇe tamasi tiṣṭhati ॥ 2.41.13 ॥
avibhāgavatāvetau guṇasāmyasthitāvubhau।
sarvakāryye buddhipūrvaṃ pradhānasya prapatsyate ॥ 2.41.14 ॥
evaṃ tānabhimānena prapatsyeta purastadā ॥ 2.41.15 ॥
yadā pravarttitavyantu kṣetrakṣetrajñayordvayoḥ।
bhojyabhoktṛtvasambandhaṃ prapatsyete yutāvubhau ॥ 2.41.16 ॥
tataḥ prapatsyate vyaktaṃ kṣetrakṣetrajñayordvayoḥ।
kṣetrajñasya pradhānasya puruṣasya prapatsyate ॥ 2.41.19 ॥
anādyau svayamutpannāvubhau sūkṣmau tu tau smṛtau ।
anādisaṃyogayutau sarvakṣetrajñameva ca ॥ 2.41.22 ॥
abuddhi pūrvakaṃ yuktau maśakau tu varau tadā।
apratyayamanādyaṃ ca sthitāvudakamapsyaśaḥ ॥ 2.41.23 ॥
pravṛtte pūrvataḥ pūrvaṃ punaḥ sarge prapatsyate।
ajñāguṇaiḥ pravarttante rajaḥsattvatamātmakam ॥ 2.41.24 ॥
satyābhidhyāyinastasya dhyāyinaḥ sannimittakam।
rajaḥsattvatamā vyaktā vidharmmāṇaḥ parasparam ॥ 2.41.26 ॥
saṃsiddhakāryyakaraṇā utpadyante'bhimāninaḥ ॥ 2.41.27 ॥
sarve satvāḥ prapadyante hyavyaktātpūrvameva ca।
saṃsarantastu te sarve stānaprakaraṇaiḥ saha।
kāryyāṇi pratipatsyante utpadyante punaḥ punaḥ ॥ 2.41.29 ॥
guṇamātrātmakāścaiva dharmmādharmmau parasparam।
guṇāstatpratidhāvante tasmāttattasya rocate ॥ 2.41.31 ॥
tadbhāvitāḥ prapadyante tasmāttattasya rocate ॥ 2.41.33 ॥
samāsādeva vakṣyāmi brahmaṇo'tha samudbhavam ॥ 2.41.35 ॥
yugapat sampravarttante bhūtānyevendriyāṇi ca।

prakriyā prathame pāde kathāvastuparigrahaḥ।
naimiṣe satramāsādya munibhyo munisattamāḥ ।
tatprasādādasaṃdigdhaṃ bhūtotpattilayāni ca ॥ 2.41.46 ॥

brahmasāyujyago bhūtvā daivataiḥ saha modate ॥ 2.41.57 ॥
bṛhaspatistu prevāca savitre tadanantaram ॥ 2.41.59 ॥
sārasvatastridhāmne ca tridhāmā ca śaradvate।
gautamāya bharadvājaḥ so'pi niryyantare punaḥ ॥ 2.41.63 ॥
niryantarastu provāca tathā vājaśravāya ca।
mayā vai tatpunaḥ proktaṃ putrāyāmitabuddhaye।

nāhitāya pradātavyaṃ pavitramidamuttamam ॥ 2.41.70 ॥
